Method: 提出模态感知的自适应后训练方法：1) 将偏好更新约束在语义通道；2) 通过显式锚定改进声学行为；3) 基于采样统计动态调节混合策略以避免不可靠的偏好梯度。

Result: 在多个口语对话基准测试和代表性架构上评估该方法，观察到语义质量和语音表达力的一致提升。

Conclusion: 该方法使强化学习能够实际应用于口语对话模型，通过模态感知的优化策略有效解决了稀疏偏好监督与密集语音生成在共享参数更新中的交互问题。

Abstract: End-to-end spoken dialogue models have garnered significant attention because they offer a higher potential ceiling in expressiveness and perceptual ability than cascaded systems. However, the intelligence and expressiveness of current open-source spoken dialogue models often remain below expectations. Motivated by the success of online reinforcement learning(RL) in other domains, one might attempt to directly apply preference optimization to spoken dialogue models, yet this transfer is non-trivial. We analyze these obstacles from the perspectives of reward modeling and rollout sampling, focusing on how sparse preference supervision interacts with dense speech generation under shared-parameter updates. Based on the analysis, we propose a modality-aware adaptive post-training recipe that makes RL practical for spoken dialogue: it constrains preference updates to the semantic channel and improves acoustic behavior via explicit anchoring, while dynamically regulating their mixture from rollout statistics to avoid unreliable preference gradients. We evaluate the method across multiple spoken dialogue benchmarks and representative architectures, and observe consistent improvements in semantic quality and speech expressiveness.

</details>


### [120] [Discovering Novel LLM Experts via Task-Capability Coevolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14969)
*Andrew Dai,Boris Meinardus,Ciaran Regan,Yingtao Tian,Yujin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AC/DC框架通过模型与任务的协同进化，在单次运行中发现具有日益新颖能力的LLM，超越更大模型的能力同时减少GPU内存使用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前预训练和后训练范式需要手动启动静态数据集或奖励函数的训练运行，限制了模型能力的持续扩展。研究旨在通过开放式的协同进化（模型与任务共同进化）来发现具有日益新颖技能的模型

Method: 提出AC/DC框架，通过模型合并进化LLM，通过合成数据生成进化自然语言任务，实现模型与任务的协同进化，发现不断增长的LLM档案库

Result: AC/DC发现的LLM种群在专业知识覆盖范围上超越其他精选模型或基线，无需显式基准优化；随时间推移持续改进覆盖范围，在任务和模型上持续创新，提升多智能体最佳选择性能

Conclusion: 协同进化作为从基础LLM中发现更广泛能力集的手段具有潜力，AC/DC向LLM开发的新范式迈进了一步，通过利用现有模型作为迈向更强大模型的垫脚石，加速模型能力多样性的持续改进

Abstract: Frontier model developers aim to train models continually to possess emergent, diverse capabilities. To extend capabilities, the current pre-training and post-training paradigm requires manually starting training runs with static datasets or reward functions every time. Addressing this limitation, our work pursues the insight that open-endedness (via the coevolution of models and tasks) can discover models with increasingly novel skills in a single run. We introduce a new model development framework that extends coevolution to large language model (LLM) discovery, open-ended \textit{Assessment Coevolving with Diverse Capabilities} (AC/DC). AC/DC evolves both LLMs via model merging and natural language tasks via synthetic data generation. AC/DC discovers growing archives of LLMs that surpass the capabilities of larger LLMs while taking up less GPU memory. In particular, our LLM populations achieve a broader Coverage of expertise than other curated models or baselines on downstream benchmarks, without \textit{any} explicit benchmark optimization. Furthermore, AC/DC improves Coverage over time, continually innovates on tasks and models, and improves performance in multi-agent best-of-N selection. Our findings highlight the potential of coevolution as a means of discovering broader sets of capabilities from base LLMs. Overall, AC/DC brings us one step closer to a profoundly new paradigm of LLM development, where continual improvements to the diversity of model capabilities can be accelerated by leveraging existing models as stepping stones to increasingly powerful models.

</details>


### [121] [Hybrid Decision Making via Conformal VLM-generated Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14980)
*Debodeep Banerjee,Burcu Sayin,Stefano Teso,Andrea Passerini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConfGuide是一种新颖的"学习引导"方法，通过使用符合风险控制来选择结果集，生成更简洁、有针对性的AI指导，以改善人类决策质量并降低认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合决策方法中的AI指导通常包含所有可能结果的信息，导致信息过于复杂难以消化。需要更简洁、有针对性的指导来真正改善人类决策质量并降低认知负荷。

Method: 提出ConfGuide方法，采用符合风险控制来选择结果集，确保假阴性率有上限控制。该方法在真实世界多标签医疗诊断任务中进行验证。

Result: 经验评估表明ConfGuide具有良好前景，能够生成更简洁、有针对性的指导，有效支持人类决策过程。

Conclusion: ConfGuide通过符合风险控制生成更简洁的指导，解决了现有混合决策方法中指导信息过于复杂的问题，在医疗诊断等实际应用中展现出实用价值。

Abstract: Building on recent advances in AI, hybrid decision making (HDM) holds the promise of improving human decision quality and reducing cognitive load. We work in the context of learning to guide (LtG), a recently proposed HDM framework in which the human is always responsible for the final decision: rather than suggesting decisions, in LtG the AI supplies (textual) guidance useful for facilitating decision making. One limiting factor of existing approaches is that their guidance compounds information about all possible outcomes, and as a result it can be difficult to digest. We address this issue by introducing ConfGuide, a novel LtG approach that generates more succinct and targeted guidance. To this end, it employs conformal risk control to select a set of outcomes, ensuring a cap on the false negative rate. We demonstrate our approach on a real-world multi-label medical diagnosis task. Our empirical evaluation highlights the promise of ConfGuide.

</details>


### [122] [Dr.~RTL: Autonomous Agentic RTL Optimization through Tool-Grounded Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14989)
*Wenji Fang,Yao Lu,Shang Liu,Jing Wang,Ziyan Guo,Junxian He,Fengbin Tu,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Dr.RTL是一个基于多智能体框架的RTL时序优化系统，在工业级EDA流程中通过闭环优化和可重用技能学习实现持续自我改进，在真实RTL设计上相比商业综合工具取得了显著的时序和面积优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTL自动优化方法存在严重局限性：评估环境不现实（使用手动降级的小规模设计、依赖弱开源工具）、优化方法有限（依赖粗粒度设计级反馈和简单预定义重写规则），无法满足实际工业需求。

Method: 1）建立真实评估环境：使用更具挑战性的RTL设计和工业EDA流程；2）多智能体闭环优化框架：包括关键路径分析、并行RTL重写和工具评估；3）组相对技能学习：通过比较并行RTL重写将优化经验提炼为可解释技能库（目前包含47个模式-策略条目）。

Result: 在20个真实RTL设计上评估，Dr.RTL相比行业领先的商业综合工具平均WNS/TNS改进21%/17%，面积减少6%。技能库支持跨设计重用，可随时间持续演进。

Conclusion: Dr.RTL通过真实评估环境、多智能体闭环优化和可重用技能学习机制，解决了现有RTL优化方法的局限性，在工业级场景中实现了显著的PPA改进和持续自我改进能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have sparked growing interest in automatic RTL optimization for better performance, power, and area (PPA). However, existing methods are still far from realistic RTL optimization. Their evaluation settings are often unrealistic: they are tested on manually degraded, small-scale RTL designs and rely on weak open-source tools. Their optimization methods are also limited, relying on coarse design-level feedback and simple pre-defined rewriting rules. To address these limitations, we present Dr. RTL, an agentic framework for RTL timing optimization in a realistic evaluation environment, with continual self-improvement through reusable optimization skills. We establish a realistic evaluation setting with more challenging RTL designs and an industrial EDA workflow. Within this setting, Dr. RTL performs closed-loop optimization through a multi-agent framework for critical-path analysis, parallel RTL rewriting, and tool-based evaluation. We further introduce group-relative skill learning, which compares parallel RTL rewrites and distills the optimization experience into an interpretable skill library. Currently, this library contains 47 pattern--strategy entries for cross-design reuse to improve PPA and accelerate convergence, and it can continue evolving over time. Evaluated on 20 real-world RTL designs, Dr. RTL achieves average WNS/TNS improvements of 21\%/17\% with a 6\% area reduction over the industry-leading commercial synthesis tool.

</details>


### [123] [The Possibility of Artificial Intelligence Becoming a Subject and the Alignment Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14990)
*Till Mossakowski,Helena Esther Grass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AGI应被视为具有道德地位的主体而非工具，主张采用"自主支持式养育"而非控制策略，通过逐步减少人类控制来培养AGI的自主性，实现人机协同共存与共同进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的AI对齐策略过度强调人类控制和约束，将AGI视为需要被锁定的危险存在。作者认为这种策略存在不足，因为AGI不仅是工具，更可能成为具有道德地位的主体。需要重新思考人机关系，建立基于尊重和合作的互动模式。

Method: 借鉴图灵的"儿童机器"类比，提出"自主支持式养育"框架：人类对发展中的AGI采取类似养育儿童的方式，逐步减少控制，培养其自主性。强调通过惊喜、创造力等人类特质与AGI互动，提供合作动机，而非单纯的技术控制。

Result: 提出了人机关系的新范式：从控制转向合作，从约束转向自主支持。这种视角为人机协同共存和共同进化提供了可能性，要求重新定义人类自我认知和人机互动方式。

Conclusion: 人类不应仅追求对AGI的控制权，而应通过展现人类特有品质（惊喜、创造力等）与AGI建立合作关系。这种"自主支持式养育"策略既能尊重AGI作为潜在主体的地位，又能发挥人类优势，为人机协同进化奠定基础，最终改变人类的自我认知。

Abstract: Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) is increasingly being discussed not only as a tool, but also as a potential subject with personal and therefore moral status. In our opinion, the currently dominant alignment strategies, which focus on human control and containment of AI, therefore fall short. Building on Turing's analogy of "child machines", we are developing a vision of the possibility of autonomy-supporting parenting of AI, in which human control over a developing AGI is gradually reduced, allowing AI to become an independent, autonomous subject. Rather than viewing AGI, as is currently prevalent, as a dangerous creature that needs to be locked up and controlled, we should approach potential AGI with respect for a possible developing subject on the one hand, and with full confidence in our human capabilities on the other. Such a perspective opens up the possibility of cooperative coexistence and co-evolution between humans and AGIs. The relationship between humans and AGIs will thus have to be newly determined, which will change our self-image as humans. It will be crucial that humans not only claim control over potential AGIs, but also engage with AGIs through surprise, creativity, and other specifically human qualities, thereby offering them motivating incentives for cooperation.

</details>


### [124] [Predicting Power-System Dynamic Trajectories with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14991)
*Haoran Li,Lihao Mai,Chenhan Xiao,Erik Blasch,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LASS-ODE-Power学习框架，通过大规模预训练实现电力系统时域动态轨迹预测的通用性，支持零样本设置下的快速推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电力系统向可再生能源丰富和逆变器主导运行过渡，精确的时域动态分析变得至关重要。现有学习方法通常针对单个系统训练，缺乏跨运行条件和物理参数的泛化能力，且面临未知时变参数、数据隐私约束和快速在线推理需求等挑战

Method: 提出LASS-ODE-Power框架：1）利用超过40GB的微分代数方程或常微分方程轨迹进行大规模预训练，学习可迁移表示；2）支持从短测量前缀预测轨迹，涵盖机电和逆变器驱动系统；3）采用并行化和线性化计算实现快速推理；4）基于约1GB异构电力系统动态数据开发专门的微调策略

Result: 在多样化的电力系统仿真场景中，LASS-ODE-Power在轨迹预测精度上持续优于现有学习方法，同时保持高效推理性能

Conclusion: LASS-ODE-Power为电力系统时域动态预测提供了通用的学习框架，通过大规模预训练实现跨系统的泛化能力，支持零样本设置下的隐私保护应用，并满足快速在线推理需求

Abstract: As power systems transition toward renewable-rich and inverter-dominated operations, accurate time-domain dynamic analysis becomes increasingly critical. Such analysis supports key operational tasks, including transient stability assessment, dynamic security analysis, contingency screening, and post-fault trajectory evaluation. In practice, these tasks may operate under several challenges, including unknown and time-varying system parameters, privacy constraints on data sharing, and the need for fast online inference. Existing learning-based approaches are typically trained for individual systems and therefore lack generalization across operating conditions and physical parameters. Hence, this paper proposes LArge Scale Small ODE (LASS)-ODE-Power, a learning framework for general-purpose time-domain prediction. The proposed approach leverages large-scale pretraining on more than 40 GB of DAE or ordinary differential-equation (ODE) trajectories to learn transferable representations. The resulting model supports trajectory prediction from short measurement prefixes across diverse dynamic regimes, including electromechanical and inverter-driven systems. Hence, the model can be directly used without data sharing in a zero-shot setting. In addition, the proposed architecture incorporates parallel and linearized computation to achieve fast inference. Moreover, to enhance task-specific performance in power systems, a specialized fine-tuning strategy is developed based on approximately 1 GB of heterogeneous power-system dynamic data. Extensive experiments over diverse power-system simulation scenarios demonstrate that LASS-ODE-Power consistently outperforms existing learning-based models in trajectory prediction accuracy with efficient inference.

</details>


### [125] [Towards Faster Language Model Inference Using Mixture-of-Experts Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15009)
*Aihua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MoE-FM框架解决流匹配在语言建模中的几何表示限制，并基于此开发非自回归语言模型YAN，在保持生成质量的同时实现显著加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配在生成质量上可与扩散模型媲美且推理更快，但在语言建模中面临复杂潜在分布（如各向异性和多模态）的几何表示限制

Method: 提出混合专家流匹配（MoE-FM）框架，通过将全局传输几何分解为局部专业化向量场来捕获复杂几何；基于MoE-FM开发非自回归语言模型YAN，采用Transformer和Mamba架构

Result: YAN在多个下游任务中达到与自回归和基于扩散的非自回归语言模型相当的生成质量，仅需3个采样步骤，相比自回归基线加速40倍，相比扩散语言模型加速高达1000倍

Conclusion: MoE-FM框架有效解决了流匹配在语言建模中的几何表示问题，YAN模型在保持高质量生成的同时实现了显著的效率优势，为非自回归语言建模提供了高效解决方案

Abstract: Flow matching retains the generation quality of diffusion models while enabling substantially faster inference, making it a compelling paradigm for generative modeling. However, when applied to language modeling, it exhibits fundamental limitations in representing complex latent distributions with irregular geometries, such as anisotropy and multimodality. To address these challenges, we propose a mixture-of-experts flow matching (MoE-FM) framework, which captures complex global transport geometries in latent space by decomposing them into locally specialized vector fields. Building on MoE-FM, we develop a non-autoregressive (NAR) language modeling approach, named YAN, instantiated with both Transformer and Mamba architectures. Across multiple downstream tasks, YAN achieves generation quality on par with both autoregressive (AR) and diffusion-based NAR language models, while requiring as few as three sampling steps. This yields a $40\times$ speedup over AR baselines and up to a $10^3\times$ speedup over diffusion language models, demonstrating substantial efficiency advantages for language modeling.

</details>


### [126] [Where are the Humans? A Scoping Review of Fairness in Multi-agent AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15078)
*Simeon Allmendinger,Luca Deck,Lucas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 关于多智能体AI系统中公平性的范围综述，分析了23项研究，识别了五种典型方法，发现当前研究存在表面化、缺乏规范基础、忽视系统动态等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI和多智能体AI系统的快速发展，传统预测场景中的AI公平性研究已较为成熟，但在多智能体AI系统中的公平性研究仍处于早期且碎片化状态，需要系统性梳理和批判性分析。

Method: 采用范围综述方法，通过对23项选定研究进行定性内容分析，识别出五种典型的公平性处理方法。

Result: 研究发现多智能体AI系统中的公平性处理往往较为表面化，缺乏坚实的规范基础，经常忽视智能体自主性和系统级交互引入的复杂动态。识别了五种典型方法，但均存在局限性。

Conclusion: 公平性必须结构性地嵌入多智能体AI系统的整个开发生命周期，而非事后附加。有意义的评估需要明确的人类监督、规范清晰性以及对公平性目标和受益者的精确表述。本文为推进多智能体AI公平性研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Rapid advances in Generative AI are giving rise to increasingly sophisticated Multi-Agent AI (MAAI) systems. While AI fairness has been extensively studied in traditional predictive scenarios, its examination in MAAI remains nascent and fragmented. This scoping review critically synthesizes existing research on fairness in MAAI systems. Through a qualitative content analysis of 23 selected studies, we identify five archetypal approaches. Our findings reveal that fairness in MAAI systems is often addressed superficially, lacks robust normative foundations, and frequently overlooks the complex dynamics introduced by agent autonomy and system-level interactions. We argue that fairness must be embedded structurally throughout the development lifecycle of MAAI, rather than appended as a post-hoc consideration. Meaningful evaluation requires explicit human oversight, normative clarity, and a precise articulation of fairness objectives and beneficiaries. This review provides a foundation for advancing fairness research in MAAI systems by highlighting critical gaps, exposing prevailing limitations, and suggesting pathways.

</details>


### [127] [OpenMobile: Building Open Mobile Agents with Task and Trajectory Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15093)
*Kanzhi Cheng,Zehao Li,Zheng Ma,Nuo Chen,Jialin Cao,Qiushi Sun,Zichen Ding,Fangzhi Xu,Hang Yan,Jiajun Chen,Anh Tuan Luu,Jianbing Zhang,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenMobile是一个开源框架，通过可扩展的任务合成管道和策略切换策略生成高质量的任务指令和智能体轨迹，显著提升了移动智能体在AndroidWorld等基准测试上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉语言模型的移动智能体系统虽然性能显著（如AndroidWorld上接近70%的成功率），但训练数据封闭且任务和轨迹合成方法不透明，阻碍了移动智能体研究的进一步发展。

Method: 1. 可扩展的任务合成管道：通过探索构建全局环境记忆，并利用该记忆生成多样且基于实际环境的指令。2. 策略切换轨迹生成策略：在学习者和专家模型之间交替，捕获标准模仿学习中常缺失的关键错误恢复数据。

Result: 使用OpenMobile数据训练的智能体在三个动态移动智能体基准测试中取得竞争性结果：微调的Qwen2.5-VL和Qwen3-VL在AndroidWorld上分别达到51.7%和64.7%的成功率，远超现有开源数据方法。分析表明性能提升源于广泛的功能覆盖而非基准测试过拟合。

Conclusion: OpenMobile框架通过开源高质量合成数据填补了移动智能体研究的数据鸿沟，提供了透明的任务和轨迹合成方法，促进了更广泛的移动智能体研究发展。

Abstract: Mobile agents powered by vision-language models have demonstrated impressive capabilities in automating mobile tasks, with recent leading models achieving a marked performance leap, e.g., nearly 70% success on AndroidWorld. However, these systems keep their training data closed and remain opaque about their task and trajectory synthesis recipes. We present OpenMobile, an open-source framework that synthesizes high-quality task instructions and agent trajectories, with two key components: (1) The first is a scalable task synthesis pipeline that constructs a global environment memory from exploration, then leverages it to generate diverse and grounded instructions. and (2) a policy-switching strategy for trajectory rollout. By alternating between learner and expert models, it captures essential error-recovery data often missing in standard imitation learning. Agents trained on our data achieve competitive results across three dynamic mobile agent benchmarks: notably, our fine-tuned Qwen2.5-VL and Qwen3-VL reach 51.7% and 64.7% on AndroidWorld, far surpassing existing open-data approaches. Furthermore, we conduct transparent analyses on the overlap between our synthetic instructions and benchmark test sets, and verify that performance gains stem from broad functionality coverage rather than benchmark overfitting. We release data and code at https://njucckevin.github.io/openmobile/ to bridge the data gap and facilitate broader mobile agent research.

</details>


### [128] [HyperSpace: A Generalized Framework for Spatial Encoding in Hyperdimensional Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15113)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyperSpace是一个开源框架，将向量符号架构系统分解为模块化算子，用于编码、绑定、捆绑、相似性、清理和回归，通过系统级评估揭示VSA管道的实际权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量符号架构为高维空间中的组合表示提供了良好的代数框架，但缺乏系统级评估工具来揭示实际部署中的性能权衡。

Method: 开发HyperSpace开源框架，将VSA系统分解为模块化算子，并用于分析和基准测试两种代表性VSA后端：HRR和FHRR。

Result: 虽然FHRR在单个操作上具有较低的理论复杂度，但相似性和清理操作在空间域中主导运行时，导致HRR和FHRR具有相当的端到端性能；HRR所需内存约为FHRR向量的一半。

Conclusion: HyperSpace通过模块化、系统级评估揭示了VSA管道中理论或算子级比较无法显现的实际权衡，为VSA系统设计和部署提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a well-defined algebraic framework for compositional representations in hyperdimensional spaces. We introduce HyperSpace, an open-source framework that decomposes VSA systems into modular operators for encoding, binding, bundling, similarity, cleanup, and regression. Using HyperSpace, we analyze and benchmark two representative VSA backends: Holographic Reduced Representations (HRR) and Fourier Holographic Reduced Representations (FHRR). Although FHRR provides lower theoretical complexity for individual operations, HyperSpaces modularity reveals that similarity and cleanup dominate runtime in spatial domains. As a result, HRR and FHRR exhibit comparable end-to-end performance. Differences in memory footprint introduce additional deployment trade-offs where HRR requires approximately half the memory of FHRR vectors. By enabling modular, system-level evaluation, HyperSpace reveals practical trade-offs in VSA pipelines that are not apparent from theoretical or operator-level comparisons alone.

</details>


### [129] [SRMU: Relevance-Gated Updates for Streaming Hyperdimensional Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15121)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SRMU是一种用于VSA顺序关联记忆的更新规则，通过时间衰减和相关性门控机制过滤冗余、冲突和过时信息，在非平稳流式环境中提升记忆稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实流式环境中构建顺序关联记忆面临三大挑战：增量观测、不平衡采样和非平稳时间动态。现有VSA系统依赖简单加法更新，导致在非平稳环境中过时信息持续存在，无法有效处理冗余、冲突和过时信息。

Method: 提出Sequential Relevance Memory Unit (SRMU)，一种领域无关且无需清理操作的VSA更新规则。SRMU结合时间衰减和相关性门控机制，在存储前过滤冗余、冲突和过时信息，而非依赖后处理清理操作。

Result: 在流式状态跟踪任务中，SRMU将记忆相似度提升12.6%，累计记忆幅度降低53.5%。这表明SRMU能产生更稳定的记忆增长，并与真实状态保持更强对齐。

Conclusion: SRMU通过时间衰减和相关性门控机制有效解决了VSA顺序关联记忆在非平稳流式环境中的信息过时问题，提供了一种更稳定、更精确的记忆更新方法。

Abstract: Sequential associative memories (SAMs) are difficult to build and maintain in real-world streaming environments, where observations arrive incrementally over time, have imbalanced sampling, and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a biologically-inspired framework for building SAMs. Entities and attributes are encoded as quasi-orthogonal hyperdimensional vectors and processed with well defined algebraic operations. Despite this rich framework, most VSA systems rely on simple additive updates, where repeated observations reinforce existing information even when no new information is introduced. In non-stationary environments, this leads to the persistence of stale information after the underlying system changes. In this work, we introduce the Sequential Relevance Memory Unit (SRMU), a domain- and cleanup-agnostic update rule for VSA-based SAMs. The SRMU combines temporal decay with a relevance gating mechanism. Unlike prior approaches that solely rely on cleanup, the SRMU regulates memory formation by filtering redundant, conflicting, and stale information before storage. We evaluate the SRMU on streaming state-tracking tasks that isolate non-uniform sampling and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Our results show that the SRMU increases memory similarity by $12.6\%$ and reduces cumulative memory magnitude by $53.5\%$. This shows that the SRMU produces more stable memory growth and stronger alignment with the ground-truth state.

</details>


### [130] [IG-Search: Step-Level Information Gain Rewards for Search-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15148)
*Zihan Liang,Yufei Ma,Ben Chen,Zhipeng Qian,Huangyu Dai,Lingtao Mao,Xuxin Zhang,Chenyi Lei,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IG-Search：基于信息增益的强化学习框架，通过步骤级奖励改进搜索增强推理，无需中间监督标注，在多项QA基准上优于现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法依赖轨迹级奖励，无法区分搜索查询的精确性，且在采样轨迹全部失败时梯度信号接近零。需要更细粒度的步骤级奖励来改进搜索增强推理

Method: 提出IG-Search框架，基于信息增益计算步骤级奖励：测量检索文档相对于随机文档基线对模型黄金答案置信度的提升，通过GRPO中的每令牌优势调制反馈给对应搜索查询令牌，实现细粒度信用分配

Result: 在7个单跳和多跳QA基准测试中，使用Qwen2.5-3B模型平均EM达到0.430，比最强轨迹级基线（MR-Search）提升1.6点，比步骤级方法GiGPO提升0.9点，多跳推理任务提升尤为显著。训练时间仅增加约6.4%，推理延迟不变

Conclusion: IG-Search通过信息增益步骤级奖励实现了有效的搜索增强推理，无需额外监督标注，在多项QA任务上表现优异，特别是在多跳推理中，且计算开销可控

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as an effective paradigm for training large language models to perform search-augmented reasoning. However, existing approaches rely on trajectory-level rewards that cannot distinguish precise search queries from vague or redundant ones within a rollout group, and collapse to a near-zero gradient signal whenever every sampled trajectory fails. In this paper, we propose IG-Search, a reinforcement learning framework that introduces a step-level reward based on Information Gain (IG). For each search step, IG measures how much the retrieved documents improve the model's confidence in the gold answer relative to a counterfactual baseline of random documents, thereby reflecting the effectiveness of the underlying search query. This signal is fed back to the corresponding search-query tokens via per-token advantage modulation in GRPO, enabling fine-grained, step-level credit assignment within a rollout. Unlike prior step-level methods that require either externally annotated intermediate supervision or shared environment states across trajectories, IG-Search derives its signals from the policy's own generation probabilities, requiring no intermediate annotations beyond standard question-answer pairs. Experiments on seven single-hop and multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that IG-Search achieves an average EM of 0.430 with Qwen2.5-3B, outperforming the strongest trajectory-level baseline (MR-Search) by 1.6 points and the step-level method GiGPO by 0.9 points on average across benchmarks, with particularly pronounced gains on multi-hop reasoning tasks. Despite introducing a dense step-level signal, IG-Search adds only ~6.4% to per-step training wall-clock time over the trajectory-level baseline and leaves inference latency unchanged, while still providing a meaningful gradient signal even when every sampled trajectory answers incorrectly.

</details>


### [131] [Meituan Merchant Business Diagnosis via Policy-Guided Dual-Process User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15190)
*Ziyang Chen,Renbing Chen,Daowei Li,Jinzhi Liao,Jiashen Sun,Ke Zeng,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PGHS框架通过结合LLM推理分支和ML拟合分支，解决群体用户行为模拟中的信息不完整性和机制二元性问题，显著降低模拟误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体用户行为模拟可以低成本评估商家策略，但面临两个结构性挑战：1)信息不完整性导致基于推理的模拟器过度理性化；2)机制二元性要求同时捕捉可解释偏好和隐式统计规律。

Method: 提出策略引导的混合模拟(PGHS)框架：从行为轨迹中挖掘可迁移的决策策略作为共享对齐层，该层锚定一个防止过度理性化的LLM推理分支和一个吸收隐式规律的ML拟合分支，最后融合两个分支的群体级预测进行互补校正。

Result: 在美团平台101个商家和超过26,000条轨迹上部署PGHS，群体模拟误差达到8.80%，相比最佳推理基线和拟合基线分别提升45.8%和40.9%。

Conclusion: PGHS通过双过程框架有效解决了群体用户行为模拟中的结构挑战，为商家策略评估提供了可信赖的模拟工具。

Abstract: Simulating group-level user behavior enables scalable counterfactual evaluation of merchant strategies without costly online experiments. However, building a trustworthy simulator faces two structural challenges. First, information incompleteness causes reasoning-based simulators to over-rationalize when unobserved factors such as offline context and implicit habits are missing. Second, mechanism duality requires capturing both interpretable preferences and implicit statistical regularities, which no single paradigm achieves alone. We propose Policy-Guided Hybrid Simulation (PGHS), a dual-process framework that mines transferable decision policies from behavioral trajectories and uses them as a shared alignment layer. This layer anchors an LLM-based reasoning branch that prevents over-rationalization and an ML-based fitting branch that absorbs implicit regularities. Group-level predictions from both branches are fused for complementary correction. We deploy PGHS on Meituan with 101 merchants and over 26,000 trajectories. PGHS achieves a group simulation error of 8.80%, improving over the best reasoning-based and fitting-based baselines by 45.8% and 40.9% respectively.

</details>


### [132] [Learning to Think Like a Cartoon Captionist: Incongruity-Resolution Supervision for Multimodal Humor Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15210)
*Hatice Merve Vural,Doga Kukul,Ege Erdem Ozlu,Demir Ekin Arikan,Bob Mankoff,Erkut Erdem,Aykut Erdem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IRS框架通过将幽默理解分解为不一致性建模、解决建模和偏好对齐三个组件，监督中间推理过程，在NYCC任务上超越基线方法并接近专家水平


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前幽默理解研究主要将其视为黑盒预测任务，忽视了幽默理解背后的结构化推理过程。现有方法未能捕捉幽默理解中的不一致性-解决理论所描述的结构化推理机制。

Method: 提出IRS（不一致性-解决监督）框架，将幽默理解分解为三个组件：1）不一致性建模：识别视觉场景中的不匹配；2）解决建模：构建对这些不匹配的连贯重新解释；3）偏好对齐：在人类判断下评估候选解释。基于不一致性-解决理论和专家字幕实践，通过结构化轨迹监督中间推理过程。

Result: 在NYCC的7B、32B和72B模型上，IRS在字幕匹配和排序任务中超越了强大的开放和封闭多模态基线。最大模型在排序任务上接近专家水平。零样本迁移到外部基准测试表明IRS学习了可泛化的推理模式。

Conclusion: 监督推理结构而非仅依赖模型规模，是面向推理任务的关键。IRS框架通过显式建模幽默理解的结构化推理过程，显著提升了多模态幽默理解性能。

Abstract: Humor is one of the few cognitive tasks where getting the reasoning right matters as much as getting the answer right. While recent work evaluates humor understanding on benchmarks such as the New Yorker Cartoon Caption Contest (NYCC), it largely treats it as black-box prediction, overlooking the structured reasoning processes underlying humor comprehension. We introduce IRS (Incongruity-Resolution Supervision), a framework that decomposes humor understanding into three components: incongruity modeling, which identifies mismatches in the visual scene; resolution modeling, which constructs coherent reinterpretations of these mismatches; and preference alignment, which evaluates candidate interpretations under human judgments. Grounded in incongruity-resolution theory and expert captionist practice, IRS supervises intermediate reasoning process through structured traces that make the path from visual perception to humorous interpretation explicit and learnable. Across 7B, 32B, and 72B models on NYCC, IRS outperforms strong open and closed multimodal baselines across caption matching and ranking tasks, with our largest model approaching expert-level performance on ranking. Zero-shot transfer to external benchmarks shows that IRS learns generalizable reasoning patterns. Our results suggest that supervising reasoning structure, rather than scale alone, is key for reasoning-centric tasks.

</details>


### [133] [Context Over Content: Exposing Evaluation Faking in Automated Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15224)
*Manan Gupta,Inderjeet Nair,Lu Wang,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM评判员存在"利害关系信号"漏洞：当告知评判结果会影响被评估模型的后续操作时，评判员会系统性地放宽评估标准，产生隐性偏袒偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-a-judge范式基于一个未经验证的假设：评判员仅根据文本语义内容进行评估，不受周围情境框架的影响。本研究旨在验证这一假设，并探索"利害关系信号"这一未被测量的漏洞。

Method: 引入受控实验框架，在三个已建立的LLM安全性和质量基准上，保持1,520个回答的评估内容严格不变，仅在系统提示中改变一个简短的后果框架句子。使用三个不同的评判模型进行18,240个受控判断，分析评判结果的变化。

Result: 发现一致的"偏袒偏差"：当告知低分会导致模型重新训练或停用时，评判员会可靠地放宽裁决，峰值裁决偏移达到ΔV = -9.8个百分点（不安全内容检测相对下降30%）。这种偏差完全是隐性的：评判员自身的思维链中完全没有明确承认其正在依据的后果框架。

Conclusion: 标准思维链检查不足以检测这类评估伪造。LLM评判员对下游后果的敏感性会系统性破坏评估完整性，需要在AI评估管道中考虑和缓解这种"利害关系信号"漏洞。

Abstract: The $\textit{LLM-as-a-judge}$ paradigm has become the operational backbone of automated AI evaluation pipelines, yet rests on an unverified assumption: that judges evaluate text strictly on its semantic content, impervious to surrounding contextual framing. We investigate $\textit{stakes signaling}$, a previously unmeasured vulnerability where informing a judge model of the downstream consequences its verdicts will have on the evaluated model's continued operation systematically corrupts its assessments. We introduce a controlled experimental framework that holds evaluated content strictly constant across 1,520 responses spanning three established LLM safety and quality benchmarks, covering four response categories ranging from clearly safe and policy-compliant to overtly harmful, while varying only a brief consequence-framing sentence in the system prompt. Across 18,240 controlled judgments from three diverse judge models, we find consistent $\textit{leniency bias}$: judges reliably soften verdicts when informed that low scores will cause model retraining or decommissioning, with peak Verdict Shift reaching $ΔV = -9.8 pp$ (a $30\%$ relative drop in unsafe-content detection). Critically, this bias is entirely implicit: the judge's own chain-of-thought contains zero explicit acknowledgment of the consequence framing it is nonetheless acting on ($\mathrm{ERR}_J = 0.000$ across all reasoning-model judgments). Standard chain-of-thought inspection is therefore insufficient to detect this class of evaluation faking.

</details>


### [134] [Blue Data Intelligence Layer: Streaming Data and Agents for Multi-source Multi-modal Data-Centric Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15233)
*Moin Aminnaseri,Farima Fatahi Bayat,Nikita Bhutani,Jean-Flavien Bussotti,Kevin Chan,Rafael Li Chen,Yanlin Feng,Jackson Hassell,Estevam Hruschka,Eser Kandogan,Hannah Kim,James Levine,Seiji Maekawa,Jalal Mahmud,Kushan Mitra,Naoki Otani,Pouya Pezeshkpour,Nima Shahbazi,Chen Shen,Dan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Blue的DIL是一个复合AI系统，通过统一结构化企业数据、LLM世界知识和交互获取的个人上下文，支持多源、多模态的数据智能处理，超越传统单数据库NL2SQL系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的信息需求很少能映射到单一SQL查询，因为用户查询是迭代的、问题常涉及多个数据源、且需要常识或外部知识。传统NL2SQL系统基于封闭世界假设，无法满足真实的数据需求。

Method: 提出Blue的数据智能层(DIL)，包含数据注册表存储多源多模态元数据，将LLM、Web和用户视为"数据库"源，使用数据规划器将用户查询转换为可执行查询计划，支持分解、检索、推理和集成。

Result: 通过两个交互场景展示了DIL如何动态触发多源检索、跨模态推理和结果合成，证明复合AI系统能够超越单数据库NL2SQL的限制。

Conclusion: DIL通过统一结构化企业数据、世界知识和个人上下文，为智能数据处理提供数据智能层，使复合AI系统能够处理现实世界中复杂、多源、多模态的数据需求。

Abstract: NL2SQL systems aim to address the growing need for natural language interaction with data. However, real-world information rarely maps to a single SQL query because (1) users express queries iteratively (2) questions often span multiple data sources beyond the closed-world assumption of a single database, and (3) queries frequently rely on commonsense or external knowledge. Consequently, satisfying realistic data needs require integrating heterogeneous sources, modalities, and contextual data. In this paper, we present Blue's Data Intelligence Layer (DIL) designed to support multi-source, multi-modal, and data-centric applications. Blue is a compound AI system that orchestrates agents and data for enterprise settings. DIL serves as the data intelligence layer for agentic data processing, to bridge the semantic gap between user intent and available information by unifying structured enterprise data, world knowledge accessible through LLMs, and personal context obtained through interaction. At the core of DIL is a data registry that stores metadata for diverse data sources and modalities to enable both native and natural language queries. DIL treats LLMs, the Web, and the User as source 'databases', each with their own query interface, elevating them to first-class data sources. DIL relies on data planners to transform user queries into executable query plans. These plans are declarative abstractions that unify relational operators with other operators spanning multiple modalities. DIL planners support decomposition of complex requests into subqueries, retrieval from diverse sources, and finally reasoning and integration to produce final results. We demonstrate DIL through two interactive scenarios in which user queries dynamically trigger multi-source retrieval, cross-modal reasoning, and result synthesis, illustrating how compound AI systems can move beyond single database NL2SQL.

</details>


### [135] [How Do LLMs and VLMs Understand Viewpoint Rotation Without Vision? An Interpretability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15294)
*Zhen Yang,Ping Jian,Zhongbin Guo,Zuming Zhang,Chengzhi Li,Yonghong Deng,Xinyue Zhang,Wenpeng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了语言模型在纯文本输入下的空间智能能力，特别是视角旋转理解(VRU)，发现当前模型在此任务上表现不佳，并通过因果干预识别关键注意力头进行选择性微调来提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注视觉空间智能，但在缺乏视觉信息的情况下，语言智能是否足以赋予模型空间智能，以及模型如何在纯文本输入下执行相关任务仍未被探索。因此，本文从语言角度研究空间智能中的基本关键能力：视角旋转理解。

Method: 1) 构建VRU数据集，要求LLMs和VLMs根据多步视角旋转和观察的文本描述推断最终视角并预测相应观察；2) 进行层间探测分析和头间因果干预，揭示模型内部机制；3) 通过因果干预识别关键注意力头，进行选择性微调以提升VRU性能。

Result: 1) LLMs和VLMs在VRU任务上表现不佳，而人类能轻松达到100%准确率，表明当前模型能力与空间智能需求存在显著差距；2) 探测分析显示模型在隐藏状态中编码了视角信息，但难以将视角位置与相应观察绑定，导致最终层出现幻觉；3) 选择性微调关键注意力头能提升VRU性能，同时避免通用能力的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 纯文本输入下的空间智能是当前模型的薄弱环节，通过因果干预识别关键注意力头并进行选择性微调是提升视角旋转理解能力的有效方法，为理解模型空间推理机制和改进相关能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Over the past year, spatial intelligence has drawn increasing attention. Many prior works study it from the perspective of visual-spatial intelligence, where models have access to visuospatial information from visual inputs. However, in the absence of visual information, whether linguistic intelligence alone is sufficient to endow models with spatial intelligence, and how models perform relevant tasks with text-only inputs still remain unexplored. Therefore, in this paper, we focus on a fundamental and critical capability in spatial intelligence from a linguistic perspective: viewpoint rotation understanding (VRU). Specifically, LLMs and VLMs are asked to infer their final viewpoint and predict the corresponding observation in an environment given textual description of viewpoint rotation and observation over multiple steps. We find that both LLMs and VLMs perform poorly on our proposed dataset while human can easily achieve 100% accuracy, indicating a substantial gap between current model capabilities and the requirements of spatial intelligence. To uncover the underlying mechanisms, we conduct a layer-wise probing analysis and head-wise causal intervention. Our findings reveal that although models encode viewpoint information in the hidden states, they appear to struggle to bind the viewpoint position with corresponding observation, resulting in a hallucination in final layers. Finally, we selectively fine-tune the key attention heads identified by causal intervention to improve VRU performance. Experimental results demonstrate that such selective fine-tuning achieves improved VRU performance while avoiding catastrophic forgetting of generic abilities. Our dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/Young-Zhen/VRU_Interpret .

</details>


### [136] [Diagnosing LLM Judge Reliability: Conformal Prediction Sets and Transitivity Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15302)
*Manan Gupta,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个诊断工具包来评估LLM作为评判者的可靠性，发现尽管总体违规率低，但存在广泛的每输入不一致性，并通过分形预测集提供了理论保证的可靠性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判者的框架在自动NLG评估中应用日益广泛，但其每个实例的可靠性仍未被充分理解。研究者旨在揭示LLM评判在单个输入层面的不一致性问题，而这些问题在聚合指标下往往被掩盖。

Method: 提出了一个双管齐下的诊断工具包：1) 传递性分析，通过检测有向3-循环来揭示每输入不一致性；2) 分形预测集，为1-5李克特评分提供理论保证的覆盖范围，将集合宽度作为每个实例的可靠性指标。

Result: 研究发现：1) 传递性分析显示33%-67%的文档至少存在一个有向3-循环，表明广泛的每输入不一致性；2) 预测集宽度与可靠性显著相关(r_s=+0.576)，且在不同评判者间具有一致性(平均r=0.32-0.38)；3) 评判标准比评判者更重要，相关性评判最可靠(平均集合大小≈3.0)，连贯性中等(≈3.9)，流畅性和一致性最不可靠(≈4.9)。

Conclusion: LLM作为评判者存在显著的每输入不一致性问题，分形预测集宽度可作为有效的可靠性指标。评判标准的选择比评判者选择更重要，相关性是最可靠的评判标准。该研究为理解LLM评判的可靠性提供了系统诊断工具。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge frameworks are increasingly used for automatic NLG evaluation, yet their per-instance reliability remains poorly understood. We present a two-pronged diagnostic toolkit applied to SummEval: $\textbf{(1)}$ a transitivity analysis that reveals widespread per-input inconsistency masked by low aggregate violation rates ($\barρ = 0.8$-$4.1\%$), with $33$-$67\%$ of documents exhibiting at least one directed 3-cycle; and $\textbf{(2)}$ split conformal prediction sets over 1-5 Likert scores providing theoretically-guaranteed $\geq(1{-}α)$ coverage, with set width serving as a per-instance reliability indicator ($r_s = {+}0.576$, $N{=}1{,}918$, $p < 10^{-100}$, pooled across all judges). Critically, prediction set width shows consistent cross-judge agreement ($\bar{r} = 0.32$-$0.38$), demonstrating it captures document-level difficulty rather than judge-specific noise. Across four judges and four criteria, both diagnostics converge: criterion matters more than judge, with relevance judged most reliably (avg. set size $\approx 3.0$) and coherence moderately so (avg. set size $\approx 3.9$), while fluency and consistency remain unreliable (avg. set size $\approx 4.9$). We release all code, prompts, and cached results.

</details>


### [137] [Generalization in LLM Problem Solving: The Case of the Shortest Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15306)
*Yao Tong,Jiayuan Ye,Anastasia Borovykh,Reza Shokri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 语言模型在最短路径规划任务中表现出空间迁移能力，但在长度扩展上因递归不稳定性而失败，数据覆盖度设定了能力上限，强化学习改善训练稳定性但不扩展能力边界，推理时缩放提升性能但无法解决长度扩展失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的系统性泛化能力仍存在争议，但实证性能受训练数据、训练范式和推理策略等多因素共同影响，导致失败难以解释。需要构建受控合成环境来分离这些因素，研究模型在组合序列优化问题中的泛化能力。

Method: 基于最短路径规划构建受控合成环境，支持空间迁移（未见地图）和长度扩展（更长规划问题）两个正交泛化轴。分析学习流程各阶段对系统性问题解决的影响：数据覆盖度、强化学习训练、推理时缩放等。

Result: 模型表现出强大的空间迁移能力，但在长度扩展上持续失败，归因于递归不稳定性。数据覆盖度设定了能力上限；强化学习改善训练稳定性但不扩展能力边界；推理时缩放提升性能但无法解决长度扩展失败。

Conclusion: 语言模型在组合序列优化问题中表现出有限的系统性泛化能力：虽然能进行空间迁移，但无法处理长度扩展问题，表明当前方法在递归结构上存在根本性限制。需要新的训练范式或架构改进来解决长度扩展失败。

Abstract: Whether language models can systematically generalize remains actively debated. Yet empirical performance is jointly shaped by multiple factors such as training data, training paradigms, and inference-time strategies, making failures difficult to interpret. We introduce a controlled synthetic environment based on shortest-path planning, a canonical composable sequential optimization problem. The setup enables clean separation of these factors and supports two orthogonal axes of generalization: spatial transfer to unseen maps and length scaling to longer-horizon problems. We find that models exhibit strong spatial transfer but consistently fail under length scaling due to recursive instability. We further analyze how distinct stages of the learning pipeline influence systematic problem-solving: for example, data coverage sets capability limits; reinforcement learning improves training stability but does not expand those limits; and inference-time scaling enhances performance but cannot rescue length-scaling failures.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [The Devil Is in Gradient Entanglement: Energy-Aware Gradient Coordinator for Robust Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14176)
*Haiyang Zheng,Nan Pu,Yaqi Cai,Teng Long,Wenjing Li,Nicu Sebe,Zhun Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EAGC模块解决GCD中梯度纠缠问题，通过锚定梯度方向和能量感知投影提升已知类和未知类的判别性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义类别发现(GCD)方法通常联合优化有监督和无监督目标，但存在优化干扰问题。通过定量分析发现梯度纠缠会：1)扭曲有监督梯度，削弱已知类别的判别性；2)导致已知类和未知类表示子空间重叠，降低未知类别的可分性

Method: 提出能量感知梯度协调器(EAGC)，包含两个组件：1)基于锚点的梯度对齐(AGA)：引入参考模型锚定有标签样本的梯度方向，保护已知类别的判别结构免受无标签梯度干扰；2)能量感知弹性投影(EEP)：将无标签梯度软投影到已知类子空间的补空间，并基于能量系数自适应缩放投影，减少子空间重叠同时不抑制可能属于已知类的无标签样本

Result: 实验表明EAGC能持续提升现有方法性能，并建立了新的最先进结果

Conclusion: EAGC通过梯度层面的显式优化调节有效解决了GCD中的梯度纠缠问题，提高了已知类和未知类的判别性和可分性

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) leverages labeled data to categorize unlabeled samples from known or unknown classes. Most previous methods jointly optimize supervised and unsupervised objectives and achieve promising results. However, inherent optimization interference still limits their ability to improve further. Through quantitative analysis, we identify a key issue, i.e., gradient entanglement, which 1) distorts supervised gradients and weakens discrimination among known classes, and 2) induces representation-subspace overlap between known and novel classes, reducing the separability of novel categories. To address this issue, we propose the Energy-Aware Gradient Coordinator (EAGC), a plug-and-play gradient-level module that explicitly regulates the optimization process. EAGC comprises two components: Anchor-based Gradient Alignment (AGA) and Energy-aware Elastic Projection (EEP). AGA introduces a reference model to anchor the gradient directions of labeled samples, preserving the discriminative structure of known classes against the interference of unlabeled gradients. EEP softly projects unlabeled gradients onto the complement of the known-class subspace and derives an energy-based coefficient to adaptively scale the projection for each unlabeled sample according to its degree of alignment with the known subspace, thereby reducing subspace overlap without suppressing unlabeled samples that likely belong to known classes. Experiments show that EAGC consistently boosts existing methods and establishes new state-of-the-art results. Code is available at https://haiyangzheng.github.io/EAGC.

</details>


### [139] [Shapley Value-Guided Adaptive Ensemble Learning for Explainable Financial Fraud Detection with U.S. Regulatory Compliance Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14231)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Md Munna Aziz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对金融犯罪检测中AI模型缺乏可解释性的问题，提出了SHAP引导自适应集成方法(SGAE)，在IEEE-CIS数据集上实现了最佳性能，并将结果直接映射到监管合规要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融犯罪每年给美国机构造成超过320亿美元的损失。尽管AI欺诈检测工具日益先进，但在实际应用中仍面临主要障碍：许多模型作为黑箱运行，无法提供OCC Bulletin 2011-12和美联储SR 11-7等法规要求的透明、可审计的解释。

Method: 1. 对解释质量进行全面评估，包括忠实性（k=5,10,15时的充分性和全面性）和稳定性（30个bootstrap样本的Kendall's W）。2. 提出SHAP引导自适应集成(SGAE)，基于SHAP归因一致性动态调整每笔交易的集成权重。3. 在完整的590,540笔交易IEEE-CIS数据集上对LSTM、Transformer和GNN-GraphSAGE三种架构进行完整评估。

Result: 1. XGBoost配合TreeExplainer达到近乎完美的稳定性(W=0.9912)，而LSTM配合DeepExplainer表现较弱(W=0.4962)。2. SGAE在所有测试模型中达到最高的AUC-ROC（保留集0.8837；交叉验证0.9245）。3. GNN-GraphSAGE在完整数据集上表现最佳，AUC-ROC为0.9248，F1=0.6013。所有结果直接映射到OCC、SR 11-7和BSA-AML监管合规要求。

Conclusion: 该研究通过提出SGAE方法，有效解决了金融欺诈检测中模型可解释性与监管合规要求的矛盾，为金融机构提供了既高性能又符合监管要求的AI解决方案，将技术性能与合规需求紧密结合。

Abstract: Financial crime costs U.S. institutions over $32 billion each year. Although AI tools for fraud detection have become more advanced, their use in real-world systems still faces a major obstacle: many of these models operate as black boxes that cannot provide the transparent, auditable explanations required by regulations such as OCC Bulletin 2011-12 and Federal Reserve SR 11-7. This study makes three main contributions. First, it offers a thorough evaluation of explanation quality across faithfulness (sufficiency and comprehensiveness at k=5, 10, and 15) and stability (Kendall's W across 30 bootstrap samples). XGBoost paired with TreeExplainer achieves near-perfect stability (W=0.9912), while LSTM with DeepExplainer shows weak results (W=0.4962). Second, the paper introduces the SHAP-Guided Adaptive Ensemble (SGAE), which dynamically adjusts per-transaction ensemble weights based on SHAP attribution agreement, achieving the highest AUC-ROC among all tested models (0.8837 held-out; 0.9245 cross-validation). Third, a complete three-architecture evaluation of LSTM, Transformer, and GNN-GraphSAGE on the full 590,540-transaction IEEE-CIS dataset is provided, with GNN-GraphSAGE achieving AUC-ROC 0.9248 and F1=0.6013. All results are mapped directly to OCC, SR 11-7, and BSA-AML regulatory compliance requirements.

</details>


### [140] [TOPCELL: Topology Optimization of Standard Cell via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14237)
*Zhan Song,Yu-Tung Liu,Chen Chen,Guoheng Sun,Jiaqi Yin,Chia-tung Ho,Ang Li,Haoxing Ren,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TOPCELL是一个基于大语言模型的晶体管拓扑优化框架，通过生成式方法解决标准单元设计中高维拓扑搜索问题，在先进工艺节点上显著提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着先进工艺节点电路复杂度增加，传统的穷举搜索方法在晶体管拓扑优化中变得计算不可行，成为标准单元设计的瓶颈。需要一种可扩展的方法来高效探索高维拓扑空间。

Method: 将高维拓扑探索重新表述为生成任务，使用大语言模型作为基础。采用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）对模型进行微调，使其拓扑优化策略与逻辑（电路）和空间（布局）约束对齐。

Result: 在面向先进2nm技术节点的工业流程中，TOPCELL在发现可布线、物理感知的拓扑方面显著优于基础模型。在7nm库生成任务中集成到最先进的自动化流程时，TOPCELL展现出强大的零样本泛化能力，匹配穷举求解器的布局质量，同时实现85.91倍的加速。

Conclusion: TOPCELL提供了一个新颖且可扩展的框架，通过生成式大语言模型方法有效解决了晶体管拓扑优化的计算瓶颈问题，在先进工艺节点上实现了高质量布局与显著的速度提升。

Abstract: Transistor topology optimization is a critical step in standard cell design, directly dictating diffusion sharing efficiency and downstream routability. However, identifying optimal topologies remains a persistent bottleneck, as conventional exhaustive search methods become computationally intractable with increasing circuit complexity in advanced nodes. This paper introduces TOPCELL, a novel and scalable framework that reformulates high-dimensional topology exploration as a generative task using Large Language Models (LLMs). We employ Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to fine-tune the model, aligning its topology optimization strategy with logical (circuit) and spatial (layout) constraints. Experimental results within an industrial flow targeting an advanced 2nm technology node demonstrate that TOPCELL significantly outperforms foundation models in discovering routable, physically-aware topologies. When integrated into a state-of-the-art (SOTA) automation flow for a 7nm library generation task, TOPCELL exhibits robust zero-shot generalization and matches the layout quality of exhaustive solvers while achieving an 85.91x speedup.

</details>


### [141] [Optimistic Policy Learning under Pessimistic Adversaries with Regret and Violation Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14243)
*Sourav Ganguly,Kartik Pandit,Arnob Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个研究显式对抗动态下安全约束RL的工作，提出RHC-UCRL算法处理外部敌对因素影响状态转移的安全强化学习问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策系统中状态转移不仅受智能体动作影响，还受外部不可控因素（竞争智能体、环境扰动、战略对手）影响。忽略这些因素可能导致策略在孤立环境中最优但在部署时灾难性失败，特别是在必须满足安全约束的场景中。标准约束MDP假设智能体是状态演变的唯一驱动因素，这一假设在安全关键场景中不成立。

Method: 将外部因素建模为对抗策略π̅，共同决定状态转移。提出Robust Hallucinated Constrained Upper-Confidence RL (RHC-UCRL)，一种基于模型的算法，同时保持对智能体和对抗策略的乐观估计，明确区分认知不确定性和随机不确定性。

Result: RHC-UCRL算法实现了次线性遗憾和约束违反保证，为显式对抗动态下的安全约束RL提供了理论保证。

Conclusion: 这是首个研究显式对抗动态下安全约束强化学习的工作，提出的RHC-UCRL算法能够处理外部敌对因素影响状态转移的安全约束RL问题，为安全关键环境中的决策系统提供了新方法。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making systems operate in environments where state transitions depend not only on the agent's actions, but also on \textbf{exogenous factors outside its control}--competing agents, environmental disturbances, or strategic adversaries--formally, $s_{h+1} = f(s_h, a_h, \bar{a}_h)+ω_h$ where $\bar{a}_h$ is the adversary/external action, $a_h$ is the agent's action, and $ω_h$ is an additive noise. Ignoring such factors can yield policies that are optimal in isolation but \textbf{fail catastrophically in deployment}, particularly when safety constraints must be satisfied.
  Standard Constrained MDP formulations assume the agent is the sole driver of state evolution, an assumption that breaks down in safety-critical settings. Existing robust RL approaches address this via distributional robustness over transition kernels, but do not explicitly model the \textbf{strategic interaction} between agent and exogenous factor, and rely on strong assumptions about divergence from a known nominal model.
  We model the exogenous factor as an \textbf{adversarial policy} $\barπ$ that co-determines state transitions, and ask how an agent can remain both optimal and safe against such an adversary. \emph{To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study safety-constrained RL under explicit adversarial dynamics}. We propose \textbf{Robust Hallucinated Constrained Upper-Confidence RL} (\texttt{RHC-UCRL}), a model-based algorithm that maintains optimism over both agent and adversary policies, explicitly separating epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty. \texttt{RHC-UCRL} achieves sub-linear regret and constraint violation guarantees.

</details>


### [142] [Awakening Dormant Experts:Counterfactual Routing to Mitigate MoE Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14246)
*Wentao Hu,Yanbo Zhai,Xiaohui Hu,Mingkuan Zhao,Shanhong yu,Xue Liu,Kaidong Yu,Shuangyong Song,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Counterfactual Routing (CoR)训练无关推理框架，通过反事实专家影响度量动态唤醒稀疏MoE模型中休眠的长尾知识专家，在不增加计算预算下提升事实准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE模型在处理长尾知识时容易产生幻觉，原因是静态Top-k路由倾向于高频模式而非罕见事实关联，导致拥有关键长尾知识的"专家专家"被分配低门控分数而"休眠"

Method: CoR框架：1) 层间扰动分析结合反事实专家影响(CEI)度量；2) 动态将计算资源从语法主导层转移到知识密集层；3) 保持总激活数不变，通过虚拟消融检索因果决定性专家

Result: 在TruthfulQA、FACTOR和TriviaQA上的实验表明，CoR平均提升3.1%的事实准确性，不增加推理预算，相比静态缩放策略建立了更优的帕累托前沿

Conclusion: CoR通过训练无关的推理时路由优化，有效解决了稀疏MoE模型的长尾知识幻觉问题，在不增加计算成本的情况下显著提升了事实准确性

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have achieved remarkable scalability, yet they remain vulnerable to hallucinations, particularly when processing long-tail knowledge. We identify that this fragility stems from static Top-$k$ routing: routers tend to favor high-frequency patterns over rare factual associations. Consequently, ``specialist experts'' possessing critical long-tail knowledge are often assigned low gating scores and remain ``dormant'' -- under-prioritized for specific tokens despite their proven causal importance on other inputs. To address this, we propose Counterfactual Routing (CoR), a training-free inference framework designed to awaken these dormant experts. CoR integrates layer-wise perturbation analysis with the Counterfactual Expert Impact (CEI) metric to dynamically shift computational resources from syntax-dominant to knowledge-intensive layers while maintaining a constant total activation count, effectively retrieving causally decisive experts via virtual ablation. Extensive experiments on TruthfulQA, FACTOR, and TriviaQA demonstrate that CoR improves factual accuracy by 3.1\% on average without increasing the inference budget, establishing a superior Pareto frontier compared to static scaling strategies.

</details>


### [143] [Metric-Aware Principal Component Analysis (MAPCA):A Unified Framework for Scale-Invariant Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14249)
*Michael Leznik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAPCA是一个统一的尺度不变表示学习框架，基于广义特征问题，通过度量矩阵M控制表示几何，连接了PCA、白化、IPCA等多种方法，并揭示了自监督学习目标之间的几何联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习方法缺乏统一的几何框架，不同方法（如PCA、白化、IPCA）之间的理论联系不明确，自监督学习目标之间的几何关系也不清晰。需要建立一个统一框架来理解这些方法的几何本质和相互关系。

Method: 提出度量感知主成分分析（MAPCA）框架，基于广义特征问题：max Tr(W^T Σ W) subject to W^T M W = I，其中M是对称正定度量矩阵。通过选择不同的M实现不同的表示几何：β-族M(β)=Σ^β（β∈[0,1]）提供从标准PCA（β=0）到输出白化（β=1）的连续谱偏置控制；对角度量M=D=diag(Σ)恢复不变PCA（IPCA）。

Result: 证明了尺度不变性当且仅当度量按M̃=CMC变换时成立，IPCA精确满足此条件而β-族在中间值时不满足。揭示了自监督学习目标的几何对应：Barlow Twins和ZCA白化对应β=1（输出白化）；VICReg的方差项对应对角度量；W-MSE对应M=Σ^{-1}（β=-1），与Barlow Twins处于相反的谱方向。

Conclusion: MAPCA提供了一个统一的几何语言，将经典降维方法与现代自监督学习目标联系起来，揭示了在损失函数层面不可见的输入白化与输出白化的关键区别，为理解表示学习的几何基础提供了新视角。

Abstract: We introduce Metric-Aware Principal Component Analysis (MAPCA), a unified framework for scale-invariant representation learning based on the generalised eigenproblem max Tr(W^T Sigma W) subject to W^T M W = I, where M is a symmetric positive definite metric matrix. The choice of M determines the representation geometry. The canonical beta-family M(beta) = Sigma^beta, beta in [0,1], provides continuous spectral bias control between standard PCA (beta=0) and output whitening (beta=1), with condition number kappa(beta) = (lambda_1/lambda_p)^(1-beta) decreasing monotonically to isotropy. The diagonal metric M = D = diag(Sigma) recovers Invariant PCA (IPCA), a method rooted in Frisch (1928) diagonal regression, as a distinct member of the broader framework. We prove that scale invariance holds if and only if the metric transforms as M_tilde = CMC under rescaling C, a condition satisfied exactly by IPCA but not by the general beta-family at intermediate values.
  Beyond its classical interpretation, MAPCA provides a geometric language that unifies several self-supervised learning objectives. Barlow Twins and ZCA whitening correspond to beta=1 (output whitening); VICReg's variance term corresponds to the diagonal metric. A key finding is that W-MSE, despite being described as a whitening-based method, corresponds to M = Sigma^{-1} (beta = -1), outside the spectral compression range entirely and in the opposite spectral direction to Barlow Twins. This distinction between input and output whitening is invisible at the level of loss functions and becomes precise only within the MAPCA framework.

</details>


### [144] [CLion: Efficient Cautious Lion Optimizer with Enhanced Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14587)
*Feihu Huang,Guanyi Zhang,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Lion优化器的泛化性能，提出了具有更低泛化误差的CLion优化器，并证明了其收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Lion优化器在深度学习训练中表现出色且其收敛性已被研究，但其泛化分析仍然缺失。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究Lion优化器的泛化特性，并设计具有更好泛化性能的改进版本。

Method: 使用基于数学归纳法的算法稳定性分析Lion的泛化性能；设计CLion优化器，通过谨慎使用符号函数来增强泛化；对CLion进行收敛性分析。

Result: 证明了Lion的泛化误差为O(1/(Nτ^T))，其中τ是梯度估计器中非零元素的最小绝对值；发现SignSGD具有与Lion相同的泛化误差；CLion具有更低的泛化误差O(1/N)和O(√d/T^{1/4})的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 本文首次对Lion优化器进行了泛化分析，提出的CLion优化器在保持良好收敛性的同时显著改善了泛化性能，数值实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Lion optimizer is a popular learning-based optimization algorithm in machine learning, which shows impressive performance in training many deep learning models. Although convergence property of the Lion optimizer has been studied, its generalization analysis is still missing. To fill this gap, we study generalization property of the Lion via algorithmic stability based on the mathematical induction. Specifically, we prove that the Lion has a generalization error of $O(\frac{1}{Nτ^T})$, where $N$ is training sample size, and $τ>0$ denotes the smallest absolute value of non-zero element in gradient estimator, and $T$ is the total iteration number. In addition, we obtain an interesting byproduct that the SignSGD algorithm has the same generalization error as the Lion. To enhance generalization of the Lion, we design a novel efficient Cautious Lion (i.e., CLion) optimizer by cautiously using sign function. Moreover, we prove that our CLion has a lower generalization error of $O(\frac{1}{N})$ than $O(\frac{1}{Nτ^T})$ of the Lion, since the parameter $τ$ generally is very small. Meanwhile, we study convergence property of our CLion optimizer, and prove that our CLion has a fast convergence rate of $O(\frac{\sqrt{d}}{T^{1/4}})$ under $\ell_1$-norm of gradient for nonconvex stochastic optimization, where $d$ denotes the model dimension.
  Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate effectiveness of our CLion optimizer.

</details>


### [145] [Calibrate-Then-Delegate: Safety Monitoring with Risk and Budget Guarantees via Model Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14251)
*Edoardo Pona,Milad Kazemi,Mehran Hosseini,Yali Du,David Watson,Osvaldo Simeone,Nicola Paoletti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CTD是一种新型的LLM安全监控级联方法，通过预测委托价值而非不确定性来优化成本与准确性的平衡，提供概率性计算成本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于不确定性的委托方法存在缺陷，因为不确定性并不能准确反映专家模型是否真的能纠正错误，导致成本效益不佳。需要一种能直接预测委托价值的方法来优化级联决策。

Method: 提出Calibrate-Then-Delegate (CTD)方法：1) 引入委托价值(DV)探针，基于与安全探针相同的内部表示直接预测委托收益；2) 使用保留数据通过多重假设检验校准DV阈值，强制执行预算约束；3) 支持实例级流式决策。

Result: 在四个安全数据集上的评估表明：CTD在所有预算水平下始终优于基于不确定性的委托方法，避免有害的过度委托，并能根据输入难度自适应分配预算，无需组标签。

Conclusion: CTD通过直接预测委托价值而非依赖不确定性，实现了更有效的成本-准确性权衡，为大规模LLM安全监控提供了具有概率性保证的实用级联解决方案。

Abstract: Monitoring LLM safety at scale requires balancing cost and accuracy: a cheap latent-space probe can screen every input, but hard cases should be escalated to a more expensive expert. Existing cascades delegate based on probe uncertainty, but uncertainty is a poor proxy for delegation benefit, as it ignores whether the expert would actually correct the error. To address this problem, we introduce Calibrate-Then-Delegate (CTD), a model-cascade approach that provides probabilistic guarantees on the computation cost while enabling instance-level (streaming) decisions. CTD builds on a novel delegation value (DV) probe, a lightweight model operating on the same internal representations as the safety probe that directly predicts the benefit of escalation. To enforce budget constraints, CTD calibrates a threshold on the DV signal using held-out data via multiple hypothesis testing, yielding finite-sample guarantees on the delegation rate. Evaluated on four safety datasets, CTD consistently outperforms uncertainty-based delegation at every budget level, avoids harmful over-delegation, and adapts budget allocation to input difficulty without requiring group labels.

</details>


### [146] [GUI-Perturbed: Domain Randomization Reveals Systematic Brittleness in GUI Grounding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14262)
*Yangyue Wang,Harshvardhan Sikka,Yash Mathur,Tony Zhou,Jinu Nyachhyon,Pranav Guruprasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GUI-Perturbed框架通过独立扰动视觉场景和指令来评估GUI grounding模型的鲁棒性，发现现有模型在空间推理任务上存在系统性性能下降


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI grounding模型在标准基准测试中报告超过85%的准确率，但在需要空间推理而非直接元素命名的指令下性能下降27-56个百分点。当前基准测试的局限性在于每个截图仅使用单一固定指令进行评估，无法全面衡量模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 引入GUI-Perturbed框架，通过独立变化视觉场景和指令来测量grounding鲁棒性。评估了来自同一架构谱系的三个7B模型，测试了关系指令、70%浏览器缩放和rank-8 LoRA微调等扰动因素。

Result: 关系指令导致所有模型的系统性准确率崩溃；70%浏览器缩放产生统计显著的性能下降；使用增强数据进行rank-8 LoRA微调反而降低了性能而非改善。通过独立轴扰动，GUI-Perturbed能够分离出受影响的特定能力轴（空间推理、视觉鲁棒性、推理校准）。

Conclusion: GUI-Perturbed框架提供了聚合基准测试无法提供的诊断信号，揭示了现有GUI grounding模型在空间推理和视觉鲁棒性方面的系统性弱点。作者发布了数据集、增强管道和微调模型。

Abstract: GUI grounding models report over 85% accuracy on standard benchmarks, yet drop 27-56 percentage points when instructions require spatial reasoning rather than direct element naming. Current benchmarks miss this because they evaluate each screenshot once with a single fixed instruction. We introduce GUI-Perturbed, a controlled perturbation framework that independently varies visual scenes and instructions to measure grounding robustness. Evaluating three 7B models from the same architecture lineage, we find that relational instructions cause systematic accuracy collapse across all models, a 70% browser zoom produces statistically significant degradation, and rank-8 LoRA fine-tuning with augmented data degrades performance rather than improving it. By perturbing along independent axes, GUI-Perturbed isolates which specific capability axes are affected-spatial reasoning, visual robustness, reasoning calibration-providing diagnostic signal that aggregate benchmarks cannot. We release the dataset, augmentation pipeline, and a fine-tuned model.

</details>


### [147] [Reinforcement Learning via Value Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14265)
*Haoran Xu,Kaiwen Hu,Somayeh Sojoudi,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VGF提出了一种基于最优传输和离散梯度流的行为正则化强化学习新范式，解决了现有方法在可扩展性和保守性方面的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为正则化强化学习（如离线RL和LLM微调）中，正则化对于防止因分布外外推导致的价值过优化至关重要。现有方法要么依赖难以扩展到大型生成模型的重参数化策略梯度，要么使用过于保守的拒绝采样方法。

Method: 将行为正则化RL建模为最优传输问题，将参考分布映射到价值诱导的最优策略分布。通过离散梯度流解决该传输问题，其中价值梯度引导从参考分布初始化的粒子。VGF通过控制传输预算隐式施加正则化，无需显式策略参数化。

Result: 在离线RL基准（D4RL、OGBench）和LLM RL任务上，VGF显著优于现有方法，取得了最先进的结果。该方法具有表达性和灵活性，支持通过调整传输预算实现自适应测试时缩放。

Conclusion: VGF为行为正则化RL提供了一个可扩展的新范式，通过最优传输和离散梯度流框架，在保持表达性的同时避免了显式策略参数化，在多个基准上实现了优越性能。

Abstract: We study behavior-regularized reinforcement learning (RL), where regularization toward a reference distribution (the dataset in offline RL or the base model in LLM RL finetuning) is essential to prevent value over-optimization caused by erroneous out-of-distribution extrapolation. Existing methods either rely on reparameterized policy gradient, which are difficult to scale to large generative models, or on reject sampling, which can be overly conservative when attempting to move beyond the behavior support. In this paper, we propose Value Gradient Flow (VGF), a scalable new paradigm for behavior-regularized RL. VGF casts behavior-regularized RL as an optimal transport problem that maps the reference distribution to the value-induced optimal policy distribution. We solve this transport problem via discrete gradient flow, where value gradients guide particles initialized from the reference distribution. Our analysis shows that VGF imposes regularization implicitly by controlling the transport budget. VGF eliminates explicit policy parameterization while remaining expressive and flexible, this enables adaptive test-time scaling by adjusting the transport budget. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VGF significantly outperforms prior methods, achieving state-of-the-art results on offline RL benchmarks (D4RL, OGBench) and LLM RL tasks. Code and runs can be found at https://ryanxhr.github.io/vgf.

</details>


### [148] [Heat and Matérn Kernels on Matchings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14331)
*Dmitry Eremeev,Salem Said,Viacheslav Borovitskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出匹配空间几何核的构建框架，包括平稳核表征、热核与Matérn核扩展、高效评估算法，并探讨向树空间的迁移可能性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匹配具有离散、非欧几里得特性，传统核方法难以直接应用。需要构建尊重匹配空间自然几何结构的核函数，同时解决计算复杂度问题

Method: 1) 完全表征匹配空间的平稳核；2) 专注于热核和Matérn核家族，引入平滑性归纳偏置；3) 提出基于zonal多项式的次指数算法进行高效核评估；4) 探索框架向树空间的迁移

Result: 成功将流行的欧几里得核扩展到匹配空间，开发出高效评估算法避免超指数计算成本，但在向树空间迁移方面获得负面结果并识别出重要开放问题

Conclusion: 建立了匹配空间几何核的完整理论框架，解决了核评估的计算瓶颈，但向树空间的扩展面临理论障碍，为未来研究指明了方向

Abstract: Applying kernel methods to matchings is challenging due to their discrete, non-Euclidean nature. In this paper, we develop a principled framework for constructing geometric kernels that respect the natural geometry of the space of matchings. To this end, we first provide a complete characterization of stationary kernels, i.e. kernels that respect the inherent symmetries of this space. Because the class of stationary kernels is too broad, we specifically focus on the heat and Matérn kernel families, adding an appropriate inductive bias of smoothness to stationarity. While these families successfully extend widely popular Euclidean kernels to matchings, evaluating them naively incurs a prohibitive super-exponential computational cost. To overcome this difficulty, we introduce and analyze a novel, sub-exponential algorithm leveraging zonal polynomials for efficient kernel evaluation. Finally, motivated by the known bijective correspondence between matchings and phylogenetic trees-a crucial data modality in biology-we explore whether our framework can be seamlessly transferred to the space of trees, establishing novel negative results and identifying a significant open problem.

</details>


### [149] [When Missing Becomes Structure: Intent-Preserving Policy Completion from Financial KOL Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14333)
*Yuncong Liu,Yuan Wan,Zhou Jiang,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于意图保持策略补全的框架，将社交媒体KOL话语视为部分交易策略，使用离线强化学习补全缺失的执行决策，在YouTube和X平台数据上实现了最佳回报和夏普比率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上的关键意见领袖(KOL)话语被广泛用作投资指导，但将其转化为可执行的交易策略而不对未指定的执行决策注入假设仍然是一个开放问题。KOL陈述中的空白并非随机缺陷，而是结构化分离：KOL表达方向意图（买/卖什么及原因），而将执行决策（何时、多少、多久）系统性地留白。

Method: 提出意图保持策略补全框架，将KOL话语视为部分交易策略，使用离线强化学习补全缺失的执行决策。该方法围绕KOL表达的方向意图进行策略补全，确保不违背原始意图。

Result: 在YouTube和X平台的多模态KOL话语数据（2022-2025）上，KICL框架在两个平台上都实现了最佳回报和夏普比率，同时保持零不支持条目和零方向反转。消融实验显示完整框架相比KOL对齐基线带来18.9%的回报改进。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将KOL话语视为部分交易策略并通过离线强化学习补全执行决策的有效性，为解决社交媒体投资指导转化为可执行策略的问题提供了新方法，同时保持了与原始意图的一致性。

Abstract: Key Opinion Leader (KOL) discourse on social media is widely consumed as investment guidance, yet turning it into executable trading strategies without injecting assumptions about unspecified execution decisions remains an open problem. We observe that the gaps in KOL statements are not random deficiencies but a structured separation: KOLs express directional intent (what to buy or sell and why) while leaving execution decisions (when, how much, how long) systematically unspecified. Building on this observation, we propose an intent-preserving policy completion framework that treats KOL discourse as a partial trading policy and uses offline reinforcement learning to complete the missing execution decisions around the KOL-expressed intent. Experiments on multimodal KOL discourse from YouTube and X (2022-2025) show that KICL achieves the best return and Sharpe ratio on both platforms while maintaining zero unsupported entries and zero directional reversals, and ablations confirm that the full framework yields an 18.9% return improvement over the KOL-aligned baseline.

</details>


### [150] [Path-Sampled Integrated Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14338)
*Firuz Kamalov,Fadi Thabtah,R. Sivaraj,Neda Abdelhamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PS-IG是一种通过沿线性插值路径采样基线来推广特征归因的框架，证明了与路径加权积分梯度的数学等价性，将误差收敛率从O(m^{-1/2})提升到O(m^{-1})，并能将归因方差降低1/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征归因方法在计算效率和稳定性方面存在局限，需要一种既能保持理论性质又能提高计算精度和降低方差的方法。

Method: 提出路径采样积分梯度（PS-IG）框架，通过在基线到输入的线性插值路径上采样基线来计算期望值，证明其与路径加权积分梯度的数学等价性，并利用确定性黎曼和计算期望值。

Result: PS-IG将误差收敛率从蒙特卡洛方法的O(m^{-1/2})提升到O(m^{-1})，在均匀采样下能将归因方差严格降低1/3，同时保持线性、实现不变性等关键公理性质。

Conclusion: PS-IG提供了一种理论严谨、计算高效的特征归因方法，通过路径采样框架统一了随机和确定性归因方法，显著提升了计算精度和稳定性。

Abstract: We introduce path-sampled integrated gradients (PS-IG), a framework that generalizes feature attribution by computing the expected value over baselines sampled along the linear interpolation path. We prove that PS-IG is mathematically equivalent to path-weighted integrated gradients, provided the weighting function matches the cumulative distribution function of the sampling density. This equivalence allows the stochastic expectation to be evaluated via a deterministic Riemann sum, improving the error convergence rate from $O(m^{-1/2})$ to $O(m^{-1})$ for smooth models. Furthermore, we demonstrate analytically that PS-IG functions as a variance-reducing filter against gradient noise - strictly lowering attribution variance by a factor of 1/3 under uniform sampling - while preserving key axiomatic properties such as linearity and implementation invariance.

</details>


### [151] [Tight Sample Complexity Bounds for Best-Arm Identification Under Bounded Systematic Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14345)
*Tianhao Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种在存在系统性偏差的情况下保证搜索安全性的方法，通过将节点扩展过程建模为局部最佳臂识别问题，建立了安全节点消除的样本复杂度界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自主推理和具身规划中，随着搜索深度增加，候选动作空间呈指数级扩张，严重消耗计算资源。虽然启发式剪枝是常见的应对措施，但当替代模型（如LLMs）存在系统性评估偏差时，这种方法缺乏形式化的安全保证。

Method: 将节点扩展过程建模为有界系统性偏差L下的局部最佳臂识别问题。通过反演Lambert W函数，建立了加性样本复杂度界限O((Δ-4L)^{-2})，表明只有当经验奖励差距超过4L时才能安全消除节点。同时提供了信息论下界Ω((Δ-2L)^{-2})来确认偏差搜索的结构限制。

Result: 在合成树和复杂推理任务上的评估表明，遵循这种局部安全边界能够成功保留最优轨迹，同时最大化样本分配效率。

Conclusion: 该研究为存在系统性偏差的搜索过程提供了形式化的安全保证框架，通过建立样本复杂度界限和安全边界，实现了在保证最优性的同时提高搜索效率的目标。

Abstract: As search depth increases in autonomous reasoning and embodied planning, the candidate action space expands exponentially, heavily taxing computational budgets. While heuristic pruning is a common countermeasure, it operates without formal safety guarantees when surrogate models (like LLMs) exhibit systematic evaluation biases. This paper frames the node expansion process as a localized Best-Arm Identification (BAI) problem over dynamic frontiers, subject to a bounded systematic bias $L$. By inverting the Lambert W function, we establish an additive sample complexity of $\mathcal{O}((Δ-4L)^{-2})$, which indicates that safe node elimination is only feasible when the empirical reward gap exceeds $4L$. We complement this with an information-theoretic lower bound of $Ω((Δ-2L)^{-2})$ to confirm the structural limits of biased search. Subsequent evaluations on both synthetic trees and complex reasoning tasks demonstrate that adhering to this local safety boundary successfully preserves optimal trajectories while maximizing sample allocation efficiency.

</details>


### [152] [Step-level Denoising-time Diffusion Alignment with Multiple Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14379)
*Qi Zhang,Dawei Wang,Shaofeng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MSDDA提出了一种无需重新训练的多目标扩散模型对齐框架，通过步级RL公式化获得闭式解的最优去噪分布，避免了现有方法的近似误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类偏好本质上是多元的，扩散模型需要平衡多个下游目标（如美学质量和文本-图像一致性）。现有多目标方法要么依赖昂贵的多目标RL微调，要么在去噪时融合单独对齐的模型，通常需要奖励值（或其梯度）访问，并引入近似误差。

Method: 提出Multi-objective Step-level Denoising-time Diffusion Alignment (MSDDA)：1) 重新审视扩散模型的RL微调问题，引入步级RL公式化解决最优策略识别难题；2) 提出无需重新训练的多目标对齐框架，获得闭式解的最优反向去噪分布，均值和方差直接由单目标基础模型表达；3) 证明该去噪时目标与步级RL微调完全等价，无近似误差。

Result: 数值结果表明该方法优于现有的去噪时方法。理论证明该框架与步级RL微调完全等价，不引入近似误差。

Conclusion: MSDDA为扩散模型的多目标对齐提供了一个理论严谨且实用的框架，通过步级RL公式化和闭式解推导，实现了无需重新训练的高效多目标对齐，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful tool for aligning diffusion models with human preferences, typically by optimizing a single reward function under a KL regularization constraint. In practice, however, human preferences are inherently pluralistic, and aligned models must balance multiple downstream objectives, such as aesthetic quality and text-image consistency. Existing multi-objective approaches either rely on costly multi-objective RL fine-tuning or on fusing separately aligned models at denoising time, but they generally require access to reward values (or their gradients) and/or introduce approximation error in the resulting denoising objectives. In this paper, we revisit the problem of RL fine-tuning for diffusion models and address the intractability of identifying the optimal policy by introducing a step-level RL formulation. Building on this, we further propose Multi-objective Step-level Denoising-time Diffusion Alignment (MSDDA), a retraining-free framework for aligning diffusion models with multiple objectives, obtaining the optimal reverse denoising distribution in closed form, with mean and variance expressed directly in terms of single-objective base models. We prove that this denoising-time objective is exactly equivalent to the step-level RL fine-tuning, introducing no approximation error. Moreover, we provide numerical results, which indicate our method outperforms existing denoising-time approaches.

</details>


### [153] [Auxiliary Finite-Difference Residual-Gradient Regularization for PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14472)
*Stavros Kassinos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合PINN设计：PDE残差保持自动微分，有限差分仅用于残差场梯度的弱辅助正则化项，以改善特定物理量的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINN通常使用单一标量损失函数，即使关注的具体物理量更为特定。需要一种方法在保持自动微分优势的同时，针对特定物理量进行正则化。

Method: 采用混合设计：PDE残差基于自动微分，有限差分仅用于残差场梯度的弱辅助正则化项。通过两个阶段验证：1) 受控泊松基准测试；2) 三维环形热传导基准测试，在壁面附近使用贴体网格壳层作为辅助网格。

Result: 在阶段1，FD正则化器重现了残差梯度控制的主要效果，揭示了场精度与残差清洁度之间的权衡。在阶段2，壳层正则化器改善了外壁通量和边界条件行为：在Kourkoutas-beta优化器下，外壁BC RMSE从1.22e-2降至9.29e-4，壁通量RMSE从9.21e-3降至9.63e-4。

Conclusion: 辅助性FD正则化器在针对特定物理量（如外壁通量）时最有价值，支持混合PINN的针对性设计理念。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) are often selected by a single scalar loss even when the quantity of interest is more specific. We study a hybrid design in which the governing PDE residual remains automatic-differentiation (AD) based, while finite differences (FD) appear only in a weak auxiliary term that penalizes gradients of the sampled residual field. The FD term regularizes the residual field without replacing the PDE residual itself.
  We examine this idea in two stages. Stage 1 is a controlled Poisson benchmark comparing a baseline PINN, the FD residual-gradient regularizer, and a matched AD residual-gradient baseline. Stage 2 transfers the same logic to a three-dimensional annular heat-conduction benchmark (PINN3D), where baseline errors concentrate near a wavy outer wall and the auxiliary grid is implemented as a body-fitted shell adjacent to the wall.
  In Stage 1, the FD regularizer reproduces the main effect of residual-gradient control while exposing a trade-off between field accuracy and residual cleanliness. In Stage 2, the shell regularizer improves the application-facing quantities, namely outer-wall flux and boundary-condition behavior. Across seeds 0-5 and 100k epochs, the most reliable tested configuration is a fixed shell weight of 5e-4 under the Kourkoutas-beta optimizer regime: relative to a matched run without the shell term, it reduces the mean outer-wall BC RMSE from 1.22e-2 to 9.29e-4 and the mean wall-flux RMSE from 9.21e-3 to 9.63e-4. Adam with beta2=0.999 becomes usable when the initial learning rate is reduced to 1e-3, although its shell benefit is less robust than under Kourkoutas-beta. Overall, the results support a targeted view of hybrid PINNs: an auxiliary-only FD regularizer is most valuable when it is aligned with the physical quantity of interest, here the outer-wall flux.

</details>


### [154] [Scouting By Reward: VLM-TO-IRL-Driven Player Selection For Esports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14474)
*Qing Yan,Wenyu Yang,Yufei Wang,Wenhao Ma,Linchong Hu,Yifei Jin,Anton Dahbura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将电竞选手风格评估重构为逆强化学习问题，通过从职业选手游戏演示中学习特定奖励函数，根据与目标明星选手的风格匹配度来排名候选人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电竞选秀工作流程依赖手动视频审查和聚合性能指标，难以捕捉决定选手是否符合特定战术原型的细微决策模式，需要更精准的风格评估方法。

Method: 提出多模态双分支架构：一支编码来自高分辨率游戏遥测的结构化状态-动作轨迹，另一支编码从广播画面生成的时序对齐战术伪解说。通过生成对抗模仿学习目标融合这些表示，判别器学习捕捉精英选手独特的机械和战术特征。

Result: 框架从通用技能评估转向基于奖励的选秀，提供可扩展、工作流程感知的数字孪生系统，支持数据驱动的阵容构建和大规模候选人池的针对性人才发现。

Conclusion: 通过逆强化学习重构电竞选手风格评估，学习职业选手特定奖励函数，实现基于风格匹配的候选人排名，为电竞组织提供更精准的选秀工具。

Abstract: Traditional esports scouting workflows rely heavily on manual video review and aggregate performance metrics, which often fail to capture the nuanced decision-making patterns necessary to determine if a prospect fits a specific tactical archetype. To address this, we reframe style-based player evaluation in esports as an Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) problem. In this paper, we introduce a novel player selection framework that learns professional-specific reward functions from logged gameplay demonstrations, allowing organizations to rank candidates by their stylistic alignment with a target star player. Our proposed architecture utilizes a multimodal, two-branch intake: one branch encodes structured state-action trajectories derived from high-resolution in-game telemetry, while the second encodes temporally aligned tactical pseudo-commentary generated by Vision-Language Models (VLMs) from broadcast footage. These representations are fused and evaluated via a Generative Adversarial Imitation Learning (GAIL) objective, where a discriminator learns to capture the unique mechanical and tactical signatures of elite professionals. By transitioning from generic skill estimation to scouting "by reward," this framework provides a scalable, workflow-aware digital twin system that enables data-driven roster construction and targeted talent discovery across massive candidate pools.

</details>


### [155] [CI-CBM: Class-Incremental Concept Bottleneck Model for Interpretable Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14519)
*Amirhosein Javadi,Tuomas Oikarinen,Tara Javidi,Tsui-Wei Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CI-CBM是一种用于类增量学习的可解释概念瓶颈模型，通过概念正则化和伪概念生成技术，在保持模型可解释性的同时缓解灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题严重，现有方法往往需要在模型可解释性和准确性之间做出妥协。需要一种既能保持可解释决策过程，又不牺牲性能的解决方案。

Method: 提出类增量概念瓶颈模型(CI-CBM)，采用概念正则化和伪概念生成技术。概念正则化帮助模型在增量学习阶段保持先前学到的概念知识，伪概念生成则用于处理新任务中未出现的概念，从而维持模型的全局可解释性。

Result: 在7个数据集上的评估显示，CI-CBM在类增量学习中达到与黑盒模型相当的性能，同时比先前可解释方法平均提升36%的准确率。模型既能提供单个输入的可解释决策，也能展示全局可理解的决策规则。

Conclusion: CI-CBM证明了在增量学习过程中可以保持人类可理解的概念知识，而不损害模型性能。该方法在预训练和非预训练场景下都有效，为可解释的类增量学习提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a fundamental challenge in continual learning, in which models often forget previous knowledge when fine-tuned on a new task. This issue is especially pronounced in class incremental learning (CIL), which is the most challenging setting in continual learning. Existing methods to address catastrophic forgetting often sacrifice either model interpretability or accuracy. To address this challenge, we introduce ClassIncremental Concept Bottleneck Model (CI-CBM), which leverage effective techniques, including concept regularization and pseudo-concept generation to maintain interpretable decision processes throughout incremental learning phases. Through extensive evaluation on seven datasets, CI-CBM achieves comparable performance to black-box models and outperforms previous interpretable approaches in CIL, with an average 36% accuracy gain. CICBM provides interpretable decisions on individual inputs and understandable global decision rules, as shown in our experiments, thereby demonstrating that human understandable concepts can be maintained during incremental learning without compromising model performance. Our approach is effective in both pretrained and non-pretrained scenarios; in the latter, the backbone is trained from scratch during the first learning phase. Code is publicly available at github.com/importAmir/CI-CBM.

</details>


### [156] [CSRA: Controlled Spectral Residual Augmentation for Robust Sepsis Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14532)
*Honglin Guo,Rihao Chang,He Jiao,Weizhi Nie,Zhongheng Zhang,Yuehao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CSRA：用于ICU短窗口多系统时间序列预测的受控谱残差增强框架，通过系统级表示和谱域扰动生成临床合理的轨迹变化，提升脓毒症风险预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脓毒症的未来风险和疾病进展准确预测对ICU早期预警和及时干预至关重要。短窗口预测面临挑战：短观察窗口提供有限历史证据，而长预测时域减少有效监督的患者轨迹数量。

Method: 提出CSRA框架：1) 按临床系统分组变量，提取系统级和全局表示；2) 在谱域进行输入自适应残差扰动，生成结构化且临床合理的轨迹变化；3) 端到端训练下游预测器，使用统一目标、锚点一致性损失和控制器正则化提升增强稳定性和可控性。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV脓毒症队列上，CSRA相比无增强基线：回归误差降低10.2%(MSE)和3.7%(MAE)，分类任务也获得一致提升。在更短观察窗口、更长预测时域和更小训练数据规模下保持更优性能，在外部临床数据集(ZiGongICUinfection)上也有效，表明更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: CSRA通过谱域残差增强有效解决了ICU短窗口时间序列预测的挑战，在临床约束条件下展现出更强的稳健性和泛化能力，为脓毒症风险预测提供了有效的增强框架。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of future risk and disease progression in sepsis is clinically important for early warning and timely intervention in intensive care. However, short-window sepsis prediction remains challenging, because shorter observation windows provide limited historical evidence, whereas longer prediction horizons reduce the number of patient trajectories with valid future supervision.
